# Agents · R · part 9

1,106 entries, review-web-security to reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **review-web-security**: Reviews the exposed HTTP attack surface — path traversal, XSS, SSRF, access control on routes, security headers, cookies, application-level DoS, information disclosure — statically, plus an opt-in dynamic verification mode that proves findings against a locally-run instance. Only activate for projects exposing HTTP endpoints. ([neolao/claude-plugin-vibe](https://github.com/neolao/claude-plugin-vibe/blob/HEAD/agents/review-web-security.md))
- **review-worker**: Run mechanical verification gates — make check, cross-checks, handoff-schema validation — and return only pass/fail with a short digest. Dispatched only by review-lead; never a top-level route target. ([jean-tessier/ai-personal](https://github.com/jean-tessier/ai-personal/blob/HEAD/suites/tier-layered-teams/copilot/.github/agents/review-worker.agent.md))
- **review-worker**: Reviews one dimension over a given artifact and reports findings as location, defect, and failure scenario ([jean-tessier/ai-personal](https://github.com/jean-tessier/ai-personal/blob/HEAD/suites/tiered-team-orchestration/agents/07-review-worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **review-workflow**: Multi-agent review is the default for non-trivial changes. Every reviewer, sub-agent or direct, must load `.claude/skills/review-verification-protocol/` before reporting findings. The protocol requires reading the actual code (not only the diff), searching usages before calling anything unused, calibrating severity (Critical / Major / Minor / Informational), and downgrading net-new-code suggestion ([artagon/artagon-site](https://github.com/artagon/artagon-site/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/review-workflow.md))
- **review-workflow-consistency**: Reviews .claude/ workflow machinery for cross-file consistency: phantom references, mismatched threshold tables, broken hook wiring, stale recipe paths, glossary drift. Dispatched by /code-review. ([JetBrains/youtrackdb](https://github.com/JetBrains/youtrackdb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-workflow-consistency.md) · ★435)
- **review-workflow-context-budget**: Reviews workflow machinery for context-window budget on three axes: always-loaded surface, load-on-demand discipline, and instant per-operation consumption. Always launched for workflow-machinery diffs; dispatched by /code-review. ([JetBrains/youtrackdb](https://github.com/JetBrains/youtrackdb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-workflow-context-budget.md) · ★435)
- **review-workflow-hook-safety**: Reviews .claude/ hooks (.sh), scripts (.sh, .py), and settings (.json) for correctness, idempotency, /tmp collision safety, hook performance, secret hygiene, and JSON schema validity. Dispatched by /code-review. ([JetBrains/youtrackdb](https://github.com/JetBrains/youtrackdb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-workflow-hook-safety.md) · ★435)
